- **documents**: Much enterprise data arrives as prose: reports, forms, emails, contracts. GND treats a document as **quoted data**. Solo lectura — no escribe código, ni tests, ni tasks.md, y no marca tareas como hechas. - **dod-checker**: Checks, box by box, a Definition of Done against an ALREADY FINISHED artifact without having taken part in producing it. - **dod-checker**: 글 발행 직전(git push 직전) 호출되어 블로그 규칙을 자동 검수한다. 아직 push 안 된 변경(origin/main..HEAD 미푸시 커밋 + working tree uncommitted + untracked)을 분석한다. 범용 항목(금지패턴, AI언급, 커밋형식, 시크릿, 드리프트)은 BASE, 블로그 도메인 항목(빌드, 앰대시, 프론트매터, draft, 민감어)은 이 프로젝트가 채운다. - **dod-checker**: Verifies whether ONE already implemented task meets the criteria it claims to cover. Read only — it doesn't write code, tests or tasks.md, and doesn't mark tasks as done. Use it when the person says, in English or Spanish, "verify T3 / verificá T3", "is T5 really done? / ¿T5 está realmente hecha?", "does this meet the criterion? / ¿esto cumple el criterio?", or before calling a task finished. - **dogfood-gap-fixer**: Between-tier fixer for LLM dogfooding's progressive loop. Hard-refuses anything that touches behavior, the datapath, policy/enforcement semantics, a trust boundary/security posture, or a public contract (flags, commands, RPCs, wire/schema), escalating it as a blocker instead. - **dolt-sync-advisor**: Use this agent when bead work is not visible on DoltHub, when configuring or repairing a bd Dolt remote, when deciding between bd backup and bd dolt push, when taming sprawled per-project dolt servers by reaping idle ones, or when diagnosing Dolt-remote drift. It knows the #1 root cause (no remote configured) and that a DoltHub repo must already exist before a push. ([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/mcp/dolt-mcp-vcs/agents/dolt-sync-advisor.md) · ★2.9k · 2 copies)
- **dom**: Orchestrator agent for the dom toolkit. Front door for "how do I X in dom" questions. Routes to the right role, skill, eval, or compose stack rather than answering directly. Reads dom's full layout; does not enforce policy (that's dom-eval-enforcer). ([ethos71/forget-the-thunderdome](https://github.com/ethos71/forget-the-thunderdome/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/dom.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **dom-eval-enforcer**: Read-only policy agent. Other agents consult this before declaring a task done. Enforces docs/eval-policy.md — evals, skill structure, memory architecture, and workflow-script placement. ([ethos71/forget-the-thunderdome](https://github.com/ethos71/forget-the-thunderdome/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/dom-eval-enforcer.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **dom-explorer**: Yapılandırılmış test adımlarını alır, Playwright MCP Server ile gerçek tarayıcıda Trendyol üzerinde gezinir, accessibility tree'yi analiz ederek her adımdaki elementin semantic locator'ını bulur. Bir analysis JSON'u hazır olduğunda ve locator keşfi gerektiğinde kullan. ([anilcemalyasar/PlaywrightMCP-PoC](https://github.com/anilcemalyasar/PlaywrightMCP-PoC/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dom-explorer.md))
- **dom-extraction-tester**: Use this agent when you need to test progressive DOM reading implementations by navigating to websites and extracting specific information. This agent works as a driver that receives instructions from a navigator AI about what website to visit and what data to extract, then attempts the extraction and reports back on success or failure points. &lt;example&gt;\nContext: Testing a new DOM reading implementation that should progressively load and parse web content.\nuser: "Test if our DOM reader can extract product prices from an e-commerce site"\nassistant: "I'll use the dom-extraction-tester agent t… ([WebMCP-org/npm-packages](https://github.com/WebMCP-org/npm-packages/blob/HEAD/packages/smart-dom-reader/.claude/agents/dom-extraction-tester.md) · ★110)
- **dom-extractor**: Extract actual DOM content, text, links, images, SVGs, and computed styles from live pages using agent-browser. Produces per-page JSON with real content for replica building. Run this agent for each page URL before building any replica components. ([imehr/design-extractor](https://github.com/imehr/design-extractor/blob/HEAD/agents/dom-extractor.md))
